**[00:59:28.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000148)

**[00:59:32.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000149)

**[01:08:15.060] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000304)

**[01:08:43.300] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000307)

**[01:09:12.420] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000309)

**[01:16:31.100] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000449)  
For fiscal year 2027,

**[01:16:34.340] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000450)  
DEVDEV will focus on

**[01:16:35.600] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000451)  
strengthening its role

**[01:16:36.980] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000452)  
as the government's planning,

**[01:16:39.660] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000453)  
policy advisory, and development

**[01:16:41.820] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000454)  
coordination institution.

**[01:16:44.360] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000455)  
First,

**[01:16:46.000] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000456)  
we will intensify proactive

**[01:16:47.800] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000457)  
policy advising,

**[01:16:49.860] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000458)  
governance reforms,

**[01:16:51.400] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000459)  
and monitoring and evaluation

**[01:16:53.040] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000460)  
to support evidence-based

**[01:16:55.520] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000461)  
decision-making.

**[01:16:57.620] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000462)  
Second,

**[01:16:59.060] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000463)  
we will

**[01:16:59.840] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000464)  
update the nation's long-term

**[01:17:01.940] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000465)  
vision, the Ambition

**[01:17:03.780] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000466)  
Natin 2040,

**[01:17:05.540] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000467)  
to ensure its continued relevance

**[01:17:08.200] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000468)  
amid emerging global

**[01:17:11.760] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000469)  
and domestic challenges.

**[01:17:14.400] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000470)  
Third,

**[01:17:15.980] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000471)  
we will implement program

**[01:17:17.560] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000472)  
convergence budgeting

**[01:17:18.640] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000473)  
to strengthen alignment of government

**[01:17:21.720] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000474)  
investments and maximize

**[01:17:23.720] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000475)  
development impact.

**[01:17:25.940] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000476)  
Fourth, we will

**[01:17:27.760] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000477)  
complete the comprehensive infrastructure

**[01:17:29.840] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000478)  
development master plan

**[01:17:31.940] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000479)  
to guide strategic infrastructure investments nationwide.

**[01:17:37.020] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000480)  
We will also pursue high-impact initiatives

**[01:17:40.060] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000481)  
to address policy and regulatory constraints

**[01:17:42.760] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000482)  
to innovation,

**[01:17:44.800] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-000483)  
strengthen the capacities of local governments

114 million

**[01:29:47.760] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000932)

**[01:29:51.520] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000934)

**[01:29:57.480] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000937)

**[01:29:59.220] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000938)  
of

**[01:29:59.700] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000939)  
individuals.

**[01:30:00.000] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000940)  
Kaya ginagamit nila ang ating national ID system for authentication. Either facial recognition or di kaya yung fingerprint. So we feel, although mayroon tayong delay per timetable, one, because of the COVID, medyo nahihirapan tayo din sa COVID.

**[01:30:19.960] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000941)  
But right now, ang ating projection sa authentication versus registration, kasi sa mga national ID ng ibang bansa, tinitingnan nila ilan ba yung nag-authenticate versus nag-register.

**[01:30:32.440] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000942)  
So ang ating projection ngayong taon, mga nasa 1.5 times na-registered. So that would be about 150 million. But as we progress, aabot tayo dapat sa mga 4 to 5 authentications per registered person.

**[01:30:49.960] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000943)  
So dapat mga nasa 4 million to 5 million per year. Yun po yung datos ngayon, Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[01:30:59.380] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000944)  
On the question sa late registration, I agree sa observation. Ang registration kasi sa birth certificate, una, it's a universal right. Everybody, Pilipinos, must be registered.

**[01:31:17.260] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000945)  
That's why the Philippine Statistics Authority is really putting program for the registration. Right now, Your Honor, ang ating registered persons, mga 97% of the total population.

**[01:31:33.340] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000946)  
So mayroon pa tayong mga 3% na hindi pa na-register. So multiply again by about 114 million, so mga 3.5 million pa ang hindi re-registrado. Substantial number ay nasa BARM.

**[01:31:44.340] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000947)  
About half of the registered sa BARM.

**[01:31:47.260] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000948)  
Now, ito rin po, dahil may late registration tayo, hindi kasi natin pwedeng itigil yung late registration kasi kawawa naman yung mga Pilipino na wala silang birth certificate. But of course, may problem tayo na yung mga non-Filipinos that are registered.

**[01:32:05.320] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000949)  
Mr. Chair, with the indulgence of [unclear], can I interject?

**[01:32:09.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000950)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:32:10.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000951)  
Sa late registration, kasi matagal na itong pinag-usapan ni Park sa mga previous hearings, I raised this question.

**[01:32:14.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000952)  
Yung sa late registration, where will it end?

**[01:32:17.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000953)  
Sabi mo, walang katapusan ito. Dapat meron. Dapat darating yung time na wala ng late registration. Sa ngayon kasi, ang tanong ko, so paano yung compliance sa personal appearance, paano nyo po in-implement sa late registration?

**[01:32:29.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000954)  
Kasi dito nagkakaproblema eh. Maraming mga Chinese nationals, hindi lang Chinese nationals, even other nationals, nakakalusot na nakuha sila ng Filipino citizenship through late registration. So how can we avoid that?

**[01:32:42.220] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000955)  
Sabi mo kasi, it will be ongoing yung late registration.

**[01:32:47.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000956)  
Sa akin, the way I foresee it, sana magkaroon ng stop. Sa ngayon pa lamang siguro, I suggest, magkaroon tayo ng tinatawag ng amnesty na I'll give you one year. Lahat. Dapat.

**[01:32:56.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000957)  
Lahat ng registration by after a year, tapos na. And then the following year, tablado na lahat and then umpisa na tayo from scratch.

**[01:33:04.860] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000958)  
Tama po yun, Your Honor, Senator. Mayroon nga ang PSA na proposal for the President to issue an executive order para kunin na natin itong, ano na lang, konti na lang, 3% of our population.

**[01:33:17.260] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000959)  
Na para lumabas sila at yung mga fees, kasi minsan may mga hand runs sa late registration, may mga bayad, na para wala na talagang bayad.

**[01:33:28.780] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000960)  
And nag-introduce ang PSA two years ago, Your Honor, Senators, ng isang step para magkaroon nga ng personal appearance. At yun yung registration sa national ID.

**[01:33:41.340] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000961)  
So we issued a memo that if you are a late registrant sa birth certificate,

**[01:33:47.260] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000962)  
you must have to appear so that the national ID team can get your biometric and demographic.

**[01:33:53.600] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000963)  
But I agree with you, Senator Raffy, na kailangan talaga may time limit para at least makover yung substantial percentage.

**[01:34:01.500] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000964)  
So in that way, wala ba kayong plano na hanggang kailan matapos itong registration system natin?

**[01:34:07.440] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000965)  
We have actually a program, Your Honor, Senator, yung birth registration assistance program at nabawasan po kasi noong 2020,

**[01:34:15.180] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000966)  
ang ating late registration ay mga close to 4 million. And since then, we're able to register about 600,000.

**[01:34:23.040] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000967)  
Hinahanap namin sila, pababa na rin naman yung number of unregistered.

**[01:34:29.520] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000968)  
And as I've mentioned, the PSA actually proposed to the Office of the President for an issuance of an executive order, precisely yung sinabi ninyo,

**[01:34:38.180] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000969)  
na parang one year para supportahan lahat, lalabas yung mga hindi registered.

**[01:34:42.760] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000970)  
But at least after that, konti na lang talaga yung...

**[01:34:45.180] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000971)  
yung unregistered individuals natin. Your Honor, thank you.

**[01:34:50.640] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000972)  
Sir Chairman, yeah. Yusef Gad, Dennis, how many more national and local government units or agencies use their own IDs?

**[01:35:01.500] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000973)  
Yung baka nakakonsolidate sa national ID.

**[01:35:04.440] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000974)  
Okay. May mga agencies na gumagamit ng kanilang ID pa rin, Your Honor. Pero yung gamit, sabi ko nga kanina, yung gamit kasi ng national ID,

**[01:35:15.180] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000975)  
the authentication. So for example, yung mga social protection programs natin, like 4Ps, lahat sila, ang ginagamit na database is the national ID database.

**[01:35:25.740] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000976)  
At yung iba pa, like Dolly 2Pod, we started also, na authenticated sila using the national ID database.

**[01:35:32.460] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000977)  
Kasi nakikita namin kung may mga duplicates eh, because of your biometrics. So doon nakikita na ikaw pala yun.

**[01:35:40.120] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000978)  
And as we discussed, sa mga social protection program, we really encourage that...

**[01:35:45.180] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000979)  
the agency will utilize as the database, the national ID database, because of the biometric authentication.

**[01:35:52.600] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000980)  
Now, does the PSA have any effort to purge yung ating system of yung mga pictures or dubious?

**[01:36:03.440] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000981)  
Yes, duplicate. Not only duplicate, yung nakapremise doon sa data registration na dubious yung pag-acquire ng national ID.

**[01:36:13.820] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000982)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:36:15.180] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000983)  
Yes, Your Honor. Yung una muna, doon sa late registration ng non-Filipinos, the PSA is very proactive.

**[01:36:22.520] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000984)  
How many have already been purged?

**[01:36:24.400] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000985)  
About 1,600 po yung nabigay, nablock na namin sa system at automatically pinapadala namin sa Office of the Solicitor General.

**[01:36:33.820] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000986)  
Kasi po yung cancellation ng birth certificate ay talagang through court.

**[01:36:38.860] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000987)  
And that's why mayroon tayong CRBS, Civil Registration of Vital Statistics.

**[01:36:45.180] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000988)  
We have a bill in the Senate and we are actually requesting, kasi may provision kaming nire-request na administrative cancellation na lang yung subvert registration.

**[01:36:56.660] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000989)  
Kasi may hirap po talaga pag-court.

**[01:36:59.000] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000990)  
But we are already, automatically may investigation tayong ginagawa through PSA.

**[01:37:04.460] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000991)  
And then once ma-establish namin na hindi talaga ito Pilipino, we block them.

**[01:37:08.720] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000992)  
And we have blocked so far, close to 1,600.

**[01:37:10.560] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000993)  
Can you submit to the committee the list of those...

**[01:37:15.180] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000994)  
late registration cases na na-purge nyo na?

**[01:37:18.620] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000995)  
So we can pursue further investigation?

**[01:37:21.380] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-000996)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:37:22.940] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000997)  
To deal with these people and those responsible behind the late registration.

**[01:37:28.080] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000998)  
Because this is a big, you know, scam ito, di ba?

**[01:37:32.180] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000999)  
Yes.

**[01:37:32.880] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001000)  
Alam nyo rin naman, we've heard of 500,000 pesos per late registration of birth and so forth and so on.

**[01:37:38.720] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001001)  
But I'm more particularly concerned about the national security issue.

**[01:37:45.180] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001002)  
Kasi kung may mga sleeper agents, mga sales dito, delikado tayo, di ba?

**[01:37:53.300] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001003)  
And we're talking of thousands or even tens of thousands of these people.

**[01:37:58.620] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001004)  
Well, you can just imagine.

**[01:38:01.640] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001005)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:38:02.780] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001006)  
Every time we have the data on block, tawag namin doon block kasi binablock namin sa system,

**[01:38:08.880] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001007)  
this information are shared with our national security agencies,

**[01:38:14.060] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001008)  
including po yung Bureau.

**[01:38:15.180] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001009)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:38:15.760] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001010)  
But we can provide the Senate, the committee, on the names po.

**[01:38:19.620] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001011)  
Yes.

**[01:38:20.100] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001012)  
Earlier on, kasi nagkaroon ng issue, I was called by dating Secretary Carl Chua

**[01:38:27.220] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001013)  
because there seemed to be a problem with the PILSIS number.

**[01:38:34.060] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001014)  
Kasi sa batas, nakalagay on its face, di ba?

**[01:38:37.760] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001015)  
But I think I was told that the World Bank had some concerns about data privacy.

**[01:38:45.180] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001016)  
So tinago natin yung PILSIS number,

**[01:38:47.560] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001017)  
ang nakalagay sa ating card, on its face, yung temporary or not the real PILSIS number.

**[01:38:56.000] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001018)  
Di ba?

**[01:38:56.760] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001019)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:38:57.720] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001020)  
So how do we resolve that?

**[01:38:59.480] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001021)  
Okay.

**[01:38:59.940] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001022)  
Ang promise ko sa kanya noon, maybe we need to amend the law para maalis yung on its face.

**[01:39:08.620] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001023)  
Kasi I agree that the PILSIS number should be hidden somewhere.

**[01:39:14.580] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001024)  
Pero binibigyan niyo maliit na card eh.

**[01:39:16.560] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001025)  
Yung iba, nawala na eh.

**[01:39:18.540] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001026)  
Ako ay, you know, milagaw sa notes ko eh.

**[01:39:22.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001027)  
Kaya memoryado ko yung aking PILSIS number.

**[01:39:25.420] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001028)  
But how about those who lost that piece of paper and what they only have is the temporary or the not the PILSIS number?

**[01:39:35.600] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001029)  
Yun ang problema.

**[01:39:36.100] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001030)  
Yung nakalagay, tama po kayo, Your Honor, Senator, yung nakalagay dun sa ating card, ano?

**[01:39:42.360] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001031)  
Yung what we call the PILSIS card.

**[01:39:44.580] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001032)  
Yung PILSIS number.

**[01:39:45.160] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001033)  
But tokenized po yan siya.

**[01:39:47.040] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001034)  
Ibig sabihin at the back end, nakalink po siya dun sa ating PILSIS number.

**[01:39:52.120] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001035)  
But I agree, baka dun sa ating proposed amendment to the PILSIS Act,

**[01:39:58.500] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001036)  
ayusin na lang natin na hindi dapat naka-expose yung PILSIS number.

**[01:40:03.460] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001037)  
Kasi siya lang po yung mag-isa talaga.

**[01:40:04.800] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001038)  
Otherwise, we keep violating the law.

**[01:40:07.080] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001039)  
In a way po.

**[01:40:08.180] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001040)  
Pero nandoon naman po, wala ka print siya.

**[01:40:10.740] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001041)  
Yeah.

**[01:40:12.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001042)  
Kasi kung offline,

**[01:40:14.580] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001043)  
hindi na makikita.

**[01:40:16.440] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001044)  
Pag offline na authentication,

**[01:40:17.920] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001045)  
ginagawa lang po namin ngayon na mag-introduce kasi may bago namang technology.

**[01:40:21.860] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001046)  
In fact, one of the section dun sa PILSIS Act is the introduction of technology.

**[01:40:25.260] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001047)  
Pwede na pong ma-link pa rin kahit na offline authentication, Your Honor.

**[01:40:29.540] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001048)  
Now, another common violation of the law,

**[01:40:32.300] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001049)  
yung the requirement of a second ID.

**[01:40:36.060] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001050)  
Hindi lahat ng transactions with the government agencies as well as private institutions,

**[01:40:42.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001051)  
pumapayag na yung,

**[01:40:44.580] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001052)  
ah, singular ID.

**[01:40:46.080] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001053)  
Nangingin na second ID.

**[01:40:47.240] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001054)  
And I'll read to you, ah, section 19,

**[01:40:49.360] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001055)  
penal provision.

**[01:40:51.160] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001056)  
Meron ditong, ah,

**[01:40:52.880] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001057)  
pag hindi tinanggap without just and sufficient cost,

**[01:40:57.360] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001058)  
ah, shall refuse to accept, acknowledge, and or recognize the PIL ID or PSN.

**[01:41:03.060] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001059)  
Subject to authentication.

**[01:41:05.100] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001060)  
Meron ditong,

**[01:41:05.920] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001061)  
shall be fined in the amount of 500,000 pesos.

**[01:41:09.720] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001062)  
So, I don't think this is being implemented, no?

**[01:41:13.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001063)  
Because of the,

**[01:41:14.380] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001064)  
ah, failure, ah, in the implementation.

**[01:41:17.280] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001065)  
In the full implementation.

**[01:41:19.320] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001066)  
Yeah.

**[01:41:19.760] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-001067)  
And I don't want this to be a dead letter law.

**[01:41:22.500] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001068)  
Ah, sa ngayon po, ah, Your Honor, yung, ah, ah, Senator, yung aming strategy, ah, una, ah,

**[01:41:28.660] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001069)  
first, yung datos natin, yung mga hindi tumatanggap, bumababa na yung number.

**[01:41:33.100] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001070)  
So, nung simula kasi mataas talaga.

**[01:41:34.960] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001071)  
Pero ngayon, kasi every month po, tinatrack namin ito from the field offices and feedback mechanism,

**[01:41:41.180] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001072)  
bumababa na siya.

**[01:41:42.260] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001073)  
Ang aming munang, ah, Your Honor, Senator, is, ah,

**[01:41:44.380] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001074)  
discussion, particularly with the regulatory agency,

**[01:41:47.840] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001075)

**[01:41:49.920] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001076)

**[01:41:54.240] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001077)

**[01:41:57.720] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001078)  
Ah, yung mga agencies naman, ah, na government agencies, yun sa lahat na namin,

**[01:42:02.180] PSA Undersecretary Claire Dennis S. Mapa** (asr-001079)

initiative for the Senate

**[01:47:22.440] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001212)  
to address critical vulnerabilities

**[01:47:25.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001213)  
in the Philippine

**[01:47:26.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001214)

**[01:47:28.600] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001215)

**[01:47:30.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001216)

**[01:47:33.160] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001217)

**[01:47:36.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001219)

**[01:47:37.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001220)

**[01:47:40.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001221)

**[01:47:43.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001222)  
Because standard Philippine

**[01:47:44.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001223)

**[01:47:46.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001224)

**[01:47:50.600] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001226)

**[01:47:52.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001227)

**[01:47:56.440] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001229)

**[01:47:58.600] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001230)

**[01:48:00.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001231)

**[01:48:03.500] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001232)  
Given that the Philippines

**[01:48:04.520] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001233)

**[01:48:07.500] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001234)

**[01:48:15.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001238)

**[01:48:17.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001239)  
weakness, I am recommending to the

**[01:48:19.180] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001240)  
DOJ to explore

**[01:48:20.340] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001241)  
and find ways to coordinate

with the ALG in amending

**[01:48:24.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001243)  
all primary PSA Civil

Registry entry forms,

**[01:48:28.600] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001245)

**[01:48:36.660] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001249)  
and dual or foreign

**[01:48:38.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001250)  
citizenships. Implementing

**[01:48:40.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001251)  
these comprehensive data enhancements

**[01:48:42.500] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001252)

and national security

**[01:48:50.400] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001256)  
sectors. First, it

**[01:48:52.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001257)  
resolves the late registration crisis

**[01:48:54.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001258)

**[01:48:56.200] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001259)

**[01:49:00.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001261)  
Exact Parental Data allows

**[01:49:02.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001262)

**[01:49:30.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001276)  
for law enforcement agencies like the Bureau of

**[01:49:32.340] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001277)

**[01:59:22.080] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001569)

**[01:59:24.220] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001570)

**[01:59:29.100] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001575)

**[01:59:32.760] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001577)

**[01:59:34.960] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001578)

**[01:59:36.680] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001579)

**[01:59:38.960] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001580)

we had a recent paper on this.

**[01:59:54.020] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001588)

But,

**[01:59:55.620] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001590)  
but essentially, it's like a combination of facts.

**[02:00:00.000] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001591)

**[02:00:10.520] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001592)  
And that's why we have been asking our Philippine Competition Commission to investigate the industry.

**[02:00:21.440] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001593)  
Both in the, not just in the, I think it's not so much in the retail, it's in the trading, the wholesaling, and the importation.

**[02:00:33.920] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001594)  
That is where I think, where we see the rigidity in the prices.

**[02:00:42.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001595)  
Because we also, Congress passed the Agri-Economic Sabotage Law,

**[02:00:49.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001596)  
which includes hoarding.

**[02:00:52.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001597)  
profiteering, price manipulation.

**[02:00:56.560] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001598)  
And that is all, but that's not the complete story, Mr. Chair.

**[02:00:59.640] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001599)  
As I said, it's a complicated thing.

**[02:01:01.680] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001600)  
There is also problems with bottlenecks in the logistics that prevent markets from functioning smoothly.

**[02:01:13.380] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001601)  
And also in the way we use trade policy, I think.

**[02:01:19.100] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001602)  
As you know, because the way we have been, we make decisions on importation and I think those quantitative restrictions are affecting the way the market functions.

**[02:01:38.740] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001603)  
So the market can't effectively function.

**[02:01:43.920] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001604)  
And when you put all this together, you have that problem.

**[02:01:49.100] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001605)  
And so until now, the major contributor to inflation, food inflation, is rice.

**[02:01:56.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001606)  
And nationwide inflation, too, because of rice inflation.

**[02:02:01.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001607)  
And that's why it's critical.

**[02:02:03.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001608)  
Now, just going back to the anti-competitive market, for example, in 2020-2024, the Competition Commission, of which you used to chair,

**[02:02:17.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001609)

**[02:02:26.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001610)

**[02:02:33.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001611)

**[02:02:38.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001612)  
It's already 2026.

**[02:02:40.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001613)  
So that, too, gives you a sense of why.

**[02:02:47.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001614)  
The anti-competition intervention, I mean, forces continue to do their thing.

**[02:03:01.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001615)  
Would you have a status of that case?

**[02:03:05.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001616)  
2024 ni-recommenda, 2.6 billion, unprecedented, fine.

**[02:03:10.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001617)  
I'm sure you're familiar with that, considering you were.

**[02:03:16.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001618)  
You were.

**[02:03:17.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001619)  
You headed that commission.

**[02:03:21.200] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001620)  
Do we see any light at the end of the tunnel that cases will be filed?

**[02:03:26.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001621)  
Because the recommendation was to fine them.

**[02:03:29.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001622)  
The penalty was about 2.6 billion, but hindi pa na-enforce.

**[02:03:37.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001623)  
Go ahead, sir.

**[02:03:39.440] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001624)  
I have no information about that, Mr.

**[02:03:42.740] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001625)  
As to the current status, I was no longer the chair when...

**[02:03:47.920] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001626)

**[02:03:51.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001629)

**[02:03:54.380] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001630)  
It's an independent...

**[02:03:55.760] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001631)  
It's headed by Mike Aguinaldo.

**[02:03:59.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001632)  
Yes, yes.

**[02:03:59.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001633)  
Well, anyway, that's one concern na hindi efektibo itong PCC in enforcing its recommendations.

**[02:04:13.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001634)  
It was just a recommendation.

**[02:04:14.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001635)  
It is still pending.

**[02:04:15.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001636)  
It has not been two years after.

**[02:04:17.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001637)  
More than two years.

**[02:04:20.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001638)  
Kasi 2004.

**[02:04:21.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001639)  
So 2024, 2025, 2026 na ngayon.

**[02:04:26.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001640)  
And I believe it was July 2024.

**[02:04:30.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001641)  
So that's a concern.

**[02:04:31.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001642)  
Now, just some...

**[02:04:35.240] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001643)  
Yung 9.7% poverty levels, how did that happen?

**[02:04:41.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001644)  
I mean, parang counterintuitive.

**[02:04:45.200] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001645)  
Nag-Iran war.

**[02:04:46.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001646)  
Tumaas ang presyo.

**[02:04:47.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001647)  
Tumaas ng gasolina.

**[02:04:50.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001648)  
Mataas ang inflation ng food.

**[02:04:54.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001649)  
Bumagsak ang poverty levels from last year.

**[02:04:58.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001650)  
Was it 16?

**[02:05:01.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001651)  
15?

**[02:05:02.940] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001652)  
Let me explain.

**[02:05:03.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001653)  
Yes, go ahead.

**[02:05:04.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001654)  
Yes, yes.

**[02:05:05.160] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001655)  
I think that, yeah.

**[02:05:07.200] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001656)  
The data that we are capturing is 2023 versus 2025.

**[02:05:15.360] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001657)  
The data was 2025.

**[02:05:17.920] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001658)  
Not 2026.

**[02:05:18.920] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001659)  
The inflation in 2025 was very low.

**[02:05:23.140] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001660)  
1.6%, something like that.

**[02:05:27.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001661)  
The 9.7% inflation is 2025.

**[02:05:32.200] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001662)  
2025.

**[02:05:32.900] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001663)  
Okay.

**[02:05:33.700] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001664)  
That was the 9.7%.

**[02:05:35.300] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001665)  
And the decrease, comparing that with the conditions, economic conditions during that period, 2023 to 2025,

**[02:05:44.900] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001666)  
across income growths, or what's called income deciles, you see that households' incomes increased by almost, in nominal terms, over 20% in those periods.

**[02:06:02.620] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001667)  
It was a period, quite unusual, I would say, because it was a period of low inflation.

**[02:06:10.840] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001668)  
Income growth was quite widespread across.

**[02:06:14.900] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001669)  
Households.

**[02:06:17.420] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001670)  
So it was not an inequitable growth.

**[02:06:22.220] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001671)  
You can see the same percentage growth across the households' income classes.

**[02:06:31.420] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001672)  
And yes, and the labor market was also at its best.

**[02:06:38.820] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001673)  
It was a period when unemployment was declining, underemployment was declining.

**[02:06:43.920] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001674)  
The 2026 is a different story because the Middle East conflict, the oil price, sharp price, oil price increases, the deceleration of growth because of the scandal, partly because of scandal and the high inflation.

**[02:07:05.420] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001675)  
So some of those gains could have been eroded.

**[02:07:10.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001676)  
Wipe out.

**[02:07:10.600] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001677)  
I don't think it is wipe out.

**[02:07:13.020] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001678)  
But I don't think, I really don't think that's wipe out.

**[02:07:15.400] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001679)  
But could have been eroded by those high inflation.

**[02:07:21.140] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001680)  
And now, unemployment is also beginning to, beginning, has also increased in recent quarters.

**[02:07:30.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001681)  
Just one more question on the...

**[02:07:32.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001682)  
Yes, before it skips my mind, I follow up.

**[02:07:36.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001683)  
Where is the country headed in the next two years in terms of the, or using the following indicators?

**[02:07:43.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001684)  
Inflation, national debt, atsaka yung peso versus the U.S. dollar and other currencies?

**[02:07:53.820] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001685)  
The last one is quite difficult to know, Your Honor, because the value of the peso does not depend just on our actions, but also actions of other countries.

**[02:08:10.860] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001686)  
Now that the Fed is strengthening.

**[02:08:13.020] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001687)  
It's raising its policy rates, you would expect a lot of pressure on the peso because dollar-denominated assets will become now much more attractive.

**[02:09:06.580] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001690)

**[02:18:09.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001848)

**[02:18:11.420] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001849)  
Thank you,

**[02:18:12.580] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001850)  
Mr. Chair and Your Honor.

**[02:18:15.520] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001851)  
This is a long-standing issue.

**[02:18:18.440] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001852)  
Recall that before PSA was reorganized,

**[02:18:23.120] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001853)  
there was a Bureau of Agricultural Statistics

**[02:18:25.340] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001854)  
that collects data.

**[02:18:26.840] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001855)  
I would collect data.

**[02:18:29.620] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001856)  
But what,

**[02:18:32.200] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001857)  
and then PS,

**[02:18:33.040] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001858)  
the National Statistics Office

**[02:18:36.260] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001859)  
was also collecting data.

**[02:18:38.840] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001860)  
And those two data do not match,

**[02:18:42.160] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001861)  
do not agree by a wide margin.

**[02:18:44.820] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001862)  
And there were many studies pointing to

**[02:18:48.480] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001863)  
the need to integrate the two

**[02:18:51.820] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001864)  
by removing the data collection

**[02:18:54.720] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001865)  
for purposes of statistics.

**[02:18:56.840] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001866)  
Statistical compilation,

**[02:18:59.560] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001867)  
especially compilation in support of

**[02:19:01.840] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001868)  
the national income accounts

**[02:19:03.760] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001869)  
away from implementing agencies

**[02:19:07.060] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001870)  
so that there is a more credible

**[02:19:09.800] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001871)  
sample-based statistically valid sampling methods

**[02:19:16.260] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001872)  
applied so that the data

**[02:19:18.320] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001873)  
are nationally representative.

**[02:19:21.000] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001874)  
So that was the decision.

**[02:19:23.240] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001875)  
But of course,

**[02:19:24.760] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001876)  
over time,

**[02:19:26.840] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-001877)
