**[02:30:00.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002313)  
And I believe may mga naging, ah, uh,

**[02:30:05.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002314)  
Most of them were fruitful and it did happen, Mr. Secretary. So hindi naman nasayang yung pagbisita ng ating Pangulo.

**[02:30:13.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002315)  
My next question, sir, is would you happen to know na number, I don't know, I want to ask you this. I'm kind of surprised kasi there was a survey, sir, it says ito pong World Happiness Report 2026. Believe it or not, the Philippines ranks as the fourth happiest in Southeast Asia.

**[02:30:37.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002316)  
I don't know for what reason bakit tayo happy and dami naman yung problema natin dito.

**[02:30:43.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002317)  
Would you happen to know, ito ba yung nakarating na sa inyo, pinag-aaralan na ba ninyo bakit tayo rank fourth? Well, sa Southeast Asia. Pero overall po, sir, sa worldwide, pang 56 po tayo. Why is that?

**[02:31:01.740] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002318)  
That's one metric that some people conceived to supplement or even.

**[02:31:13.180] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002319)  
Some to replace the traditional indicators like GDP, Mr. Chair. But usually the indicators are based on perceptions. So they're not quite strictly comparable to the traditional metrics that we have.

**[02:31:43.180] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002320)  
Like employment or inflation or GDP. It's more akin to, I believe, how social weather station surveys people's perception and the state of the economy or well-being, Mr. Chair.

**[02:32:03.980] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002321)  
So, yeah, Butan is the one that has been the advocate of this.

**[02:32:13.180] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002322)  
Gross domestic happiness as a measure of well-being for countries. And there are some countries that actually want that idea, but still not part of the global mainstream for compilation of statistics.

**[02:32:43.180] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002323)  
Especially involving welfare.

**[02:32:45.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002324)  
Kasi kung titignan mo, Mr. Secretary, sa social media, kung ano-ano sinasabi, parang wala ng pag-asa itong ating bayan. Kung anong-anong paninira na politika, et cetera, mahal na kuryente, mahal na bilihin.

**[02:32:59.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002325)  
Dapat it will, ika nga, yung ating minimum wage, et cetera. So, not that far with other countries. Pero, I don't know. I would appreciate it, sir.

**[02:33:13.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002326)  
If you would look into this, I just want to find out why. Why are we ranked fourth happiest in Southeast Asia? We're the fourth, our people are the fourth happiest people in ASEAN, sir.

**[02:33:26.400] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002327)  
I will look into that and what data factors that they consider in compiling the data, Mr. Chair.

**[02:33:37.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002328)  
All right. I have another question, sir.

**[02:33:41.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002329)  
I find the bill.

**[02:33:43.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002330)  
When I came here in the Senate last year, on the lifetime validity of PWD IDs, inincorporate na po ba ang PWID sa national ID? Kasi ito pong reklamo ng PWDs. Every year or two years or three years, they have to go to the city hall, to the munisipyo. They have to travel, lalo na po yung may problema sa mobility.

**[02:34:09.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002331)  
Inconvenient nga naman. So, I filed a bill na maging gawin na po.

**[02:34:13.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002332)  
Itong lifetime. Habang nabubuhay yung PWD, tawag na siya mag-renew na mag-renew. Dahil kita naman, may kapansanang, putol ang paa, etc. na makalakad.

**[02:34:26.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002333)  
Na-incorporate ba ito sa national ID or wala pa po? Wala pang plano dito?

**[02:34:43.180] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002334)  
Mr. Chair, it's now under discussion with the National Council of Disability Affairs. I think it's with the board.

**[02:34:56.040] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002335)  
And once we have that recommendation, we'll pass it on to PSA for action.

**[02:35:01.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002336)  
All right. Thank you, Mr. Secretary. Another point I want to raise with you, your office. I also filed a bill on Philippine Oil Reserve.

**[02:35:14.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002337)  
Maaaring po bang malaman ang posisyon po ng DepDev? How will this strongly help our country's economy in terms of oil shortage?

**[02:35:23.140] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002338)  
Actually, I've been speaking to some of the representatives of ADNOC in Abu Dhabi.

**[02:35:31.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002339)  
They're very willing, Mr. Secretary, to put up storage facilities. Matter of fact, they want to put up storage facilities in Luzon, Visayas, and Mindanao.

**[02:35:41.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002340)  
All we have to do is we have to come up or we have to identify an area where they can put the storage facilities. Depende daw po sa ating kung gano'ng kalaki. And they're looking at around 50 million barrels they want to store.

**[02:35:57.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002341)  
So kung malalagyan. Pero when we were computing, it's a Department of Energy, it will take like about 500 hectares for that 50 million barrels. Because we consume like around 450,000 barrels per day.

**[02:36:15.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002342)  
Saudi Arabia is also, Aramco is also interested, Mr. Secretary, in the same deal. Bahala po tayo sa lupa, sila po ang magkukonstruct.

**[02:36:26.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002343)  
No money out po sa atin. Pati yung reserve po, yung fuel, ilalagay po nila. Well, double purpose din po sa kanila yun para kung magkakaroon na naman ng gera or something to that effect like closing Strait of Hormuz, Arabian Sea, etc.

**[02:36:45.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002344)  
Meron na po silang pagkukunan na labas sa country nila. That's what their plans are. And then, what do we get out of it will be the first priority.

**[02:36:55.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002345)  
So, number two, hindi naman totally bawaba na malaki yung price. Pero may konting effect because hindi na po itatransport kung meron na po mga storage facilities. I believe DOE also is making or talking with Japan, Ministry of Energy, METI.

**[02:37:21.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002346)  
So, yun po. Yung tatlong...

**[02:37:25.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002347)  
Aspect po na yun. Yun ang tanong ko sa inyo. Anong position nyo rito, Secretary?

**[02:37:32.320] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002348)  
I think the cost of energy and security, Mr. Chair, Your Honor, is very, very high as we have experienced it. So, we should explore options like what you're...

**[02:37:55.860] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002349)  
proposing, Your Honor. And this one that's the most fiscally and economically viable. Of course, even for oil reserve storage, there are possibly other ways of achieving that. And we need to look at what is the most advantageous, especially from a fiscal point of view.

**[02:38:25.860] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002350)  
Space viewpoint because our fiscal space is so challenging. And we should undertake such measure.

**[02:38:39.860] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002351)  
Of course, there are other things that...

**[02:38:51.860] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002352)  
Other things that we need to do.

**[02:38:55.860] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002353)  
To complement that measure, Mr. Chair, like increasing the mix of our energy. It should be seriously considered too because we are so vulnerable in having just a few sources. I'm talking about, for example, the need to perhaps even consider nuclear energy to the extent that these are

**[02:39:21.860] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002354)  
technologically manageable. And we can assure of the safety of such technology because that's very cheap also from a source of energy. And so, yeah, I completely agree. We should move into diversifying our sources and making sure that we need the power when we need the most.

**[02:39:48.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002355)  
Mr. Secretary, I believe on your next cabinet meeting, you might want to...

**[02:39:51.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002356)  
I'm going to have to inform or remind the President because I believe on his visit in Dubai,

**[02:39:56.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002357)  
he was already informed of their interest in building storage facilities. I believe Secretary Garin was also informed about it.

**[02:40:09.140] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002358)  
So, and just recently, Saudi Arabia also made a proposal wherein they want to build storage facilities. If we have all these countries' storage facilities,

**[02:40:20.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002359)  
I guess we won't have any problems. Hindi po tayo kakaba-kaba pag nagkaroon po ng mga gera in the very near future dahil nandito po yung kanilang facilities.

**[02:40:29.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002360)  
Another thing, Mr. Secretary, I want to bring to your attention. I don't know. Gusto ko sanang paimbestigahan because I'm the chairman of the Committee on Energy.

**[02:40:39.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002361)  
Unfortunately, we're in the process of conducting impeachment trial and then meron po kami budget hearing. But I really would love to get my hands on this issue.

**[02:40:50.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002362)  
About our electricity rate. I found out, Mr. Secretary, maybe your office, maybe you can help us out. You can help my committee look into this because we have a problem with our power.

**[02:41:05.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002363)  
It's one of the most expensive or the most expensive in the sea. But Mr. Secretary, when I did my research a couple of days ago, about 60% of our power, electricity, the source is coal.

**[02:41:20.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002364)  
60%.

**[02:41:20.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002365)  
About 10% to 15% is a bunker fuel. Pero how come the rate is so high, Mr. Secretary? And so I looked into it and I saw the problem.

**[02:41:34.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002366)  
We get most of our coal from Jakarta. It's like 3 pesos per kilowatt hour. Bunker fuel per kilowatt hour is like about 25, 35 pesos. So there's a big difference.

**[02:41:47.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002367)  
3 pesos po itong isa. 35 po itong isa. Pero pag dumating na po sa WESEM, sa spot market, pumapantay na po yung coal.

**[02:42:02.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002368)  
At ang reason, kasi daw yung shipment. And I don't think, Mr. Secretary, na from 3 pesos, aabot sa 35 pesos ang coal dahil sa shipment.

**[02:42:15.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002369)  
Pinapantay po. Nagkakaproblema po tayo sa WESEM.

**[02:42:17.220] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002370)  
Sa spot market. Pumapantay po yung 3 pesos na coal sa bunker fuel. I guess if we can address this issue, if we can call the attention of WESEM, and probably the ERC can help us out, control this.

**[02:42:33.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002371)  
I believe, Mr. Secretary, magiging affordable kahit papano yung electricity natin. Hindi po, sir. Ang ginagawa po sa spot market, dito po yung bunker fuel, dito po yung coal.

**[02:42:46.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002372)  
Pinapantay po nila. You get what I mean? Wala na po yung difference eh.

**[02:42:54.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002373)  
Eh dapat po mas mura dahil coal po tayo. Hindi naman po bunker. Bunker is only 15%.

**[02:43:00.320] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002374)  
We use like about 60%. So maybe, Mr. Secretary, through your office, maybe you can call the attention of the WESEM para po ikang matignan po ito.

**[02:43:12.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002375)  
Because there's really a big disparity, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:43:16.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002376)  
And if we can do something about this, I guess we can bring the electricity rate down, Secretary Balisakan.

**[02:43:24.880] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002377)  
Look into it, sir.

**[02:43:27.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002378)  
Thank you, sir. That's what we found out recently. And I was informed by my contacts in the Energy Department na yan nga daw po ang problema.

**[02:43:42.140] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002379)  
Alright, sir.

**[02:43:46.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002380)  
Under the 2025 COA report, Mr. Secretary, the NEDA Building Improvement Project, Phase 1, 2, and 3, with a combined contract cost of 641.556 million, remain not completed due to risk, contingencies, and other circumstances not adequately considered by both the agency and the contractor.

**[02:44:13.760] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002381)  
Resulting in project delays.

**[02:44:16.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002382)  
Resulting in project delays and multiple contract amendments, extensions, which affected the timely delivery of intended benefits to end-users. Moreover, payment for variation orders arising from the erroneous as found plans submitted by the contractor resulted in increased costs to the agency and further contributed to delays in project completion.

**[02:44:40.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002385)  
May project delays po ang NEDA building, improvement project with a combined contract cost worth 641 million.

**[02:44:49.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002386)  
Madaming contract amendments and extensions.

**[02:44:53.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002387)  
Naayos na po ba itong problema na ito, Mr. Secretary?

**[02:45:00.000] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002388)  
Yes, Mr. Chair, but I have my Undersecretary Monica Pagunsan here who is in charge of our building project to brief you on that.

**[02:45:18.400] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002389)  
But what you mentioned is correct. We only found later after digging the basement for the basement of the building that the structure of the building adjacent to what we are trying to build was not stable because I believe some defects in the earlier construction.

**[02:45:44.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002390)  
What is the problem initially on your investment?

**[02:45:48.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002391)  
Before we go to the Undersecretary, I don't have any problem as a design. Is it the contractor's fault trying to save money? What is it?

**[02:46:01.860] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002392)  
No, I believe what happened, the design was correct, Your Honor. But when the contractor, after digging the grounds, where the building would be put up is just…

**[02:46:18.400] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002393)  
It was adjacent to an existing building and it turned out that the walls for that older building was quite weak and unstable so that the contractor was so concerned that that might collapse and so they had to do some remediation to prevent possible collapse of the walls.

**[02:46:48.400] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002394)  
But Monica knows the details better than I do.

**[02:46:54.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002395)  
Go ahead, Undersecretary, ma'am. Go ahead.

**[02:46:57.920] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002396)  
Mr. Chair and Senator Tulfo, first of all, we have two constructions. One is for the renovation of the old building.

**[02:47:11.620] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002397)  
That was a renovation that does not really have… we don't really have visibility because we don't…

**[02:47:18.400] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002398)  
We don't have the as-built plan. So that when you go into the renovation process, you eventually find some things that were not really visible during the inspection, which really have to do an investigation.

**[02:47:36.560] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002399)  
But these are only minor, no? But we really have to correct them.

**[02:47:44.160] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002400)  
Another one is there was a delay because at that time…

**[02:47:48.400] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002401)  
We have asked for help from DPWH to retrofit that old building.

**[02:47:57.140] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002402)  
There was a… and that was a contract entered into by DPWH.

**[02:48:02.420] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002403)  
That is why when DPWH was doing the retrofitting, we did not accept the result, no?

**[02:48:16.560] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002404)  
Of the first…

**[02:48:18.400] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002405)  
The completion. In other words, we did not accept the project. That was why there was also a delay in the execution of the renovation.

**[02:48:31.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002406)  
Kaya nga, ma'am, saan ho naging problema? Lack of foresight? Kanino pong kasalanan? Is it the contractor?

**[02:48:37.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002407)  
Of course, when you do renovation, Undersecretary, there will be plans. Good thing po, nakita ho ninyo bago may madisgrasya, di po ba may mamatay.

**[02:48:48.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002408)  
Kunting lindo lang ho yan. Tama si Secretary. Baka may mga masaktan. So, it was good. Pero my question is, lack of foresight? Yung planning? O yung ikang drawing ng architect?

**[02:49:03.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002409)  
I mean, they should have submitted plans for the repair, Undersecretary.

**[02:49:10.480] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002410)  
There are really cases, Senator, that we really have to rely on as-built plans.

**[02:49:18.400] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002411)  
Unfortunately, there was none. We can no longer find the as-built plan which was done in 19… I mean, the building was built in 1972.

**[02:49:28.740] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002412)  
We cannot find that. So that, when we came up with a plan, we were not able to see certain issues that still needs to be addressed.

**[02:49:44.480] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002413)  
But really, the main delay there was that the implementation…

**[02:49:48.400] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002414)  
The implementation of the retrofitting, which we cannot also go ahead. We had to complete the retrofitting first before we can continue with the execution of the plan.

**[02:49:59.240] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002415)  
Which we consider na there was really no… The plan was alright. It's in order. However, the delay really was on.

**[02:50:18.240] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002416)  
On the retrofitting, and as well as certain areas that we need to correct in the design of the building based on the as-found when we did a deeper investigation of the project.

**[02:50:37.340] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002417)  
So, we did our best. I think there was… Our designers did… They may not have anticipated this.

**[02:50:47.680] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002418)  
However, dahil as-found… We did not find it during the design stage. Dahil it's… You really have to open up the certain areas before you do remediation.

**[02:51:05.560] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002419)  
Kasi ma'am, kung may mga… Kung ang problem po ninyo, yung pagiging stable ng building when you construct it, meron po dapat ikaw nga mga tinatawag tayo na soil.

**[02:51:17.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002420)  
Ito ba yung malambot? Kasi may mga projects po tayo, take the case of that skyway they built in Iloilo, sagging po at sumasayaw-sayaw ngayon because hindi po sila nag-soil test.

**[02:51:33.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002421)  
Kaya yung foundation ng tulay ko sa Iloilo, gumaganoon. Under investigation nga din po. Kasama po yun dyan sa flood control issue. Kasi nga po yung contractor iniimbestigahan na ngayon dahil hindi nga po stable.

**[02:51:47.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002422)  
Wala po bang ginawang soil test ang ika nga contractor o yung engineers na ika nga nagagawa po ng building na yan para ika nga masiguro na safe po na magtayo ng building dyan?

**[02:52:06.520] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002423)  
We did have a soil testing, Mr. Chair, but really… And we did even go into the investigation of the entire building, of the old building.

**[02:52:17.680] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002424)  
And that was also the basis for the design, for the structural design of the building.

**[02:52:23.740] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002425)  
It's just that naabutan po kami. We had to retrofit and strengthen the foundation of the old building.

**[02:52:37.880] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002426)  
Yung nare-refer po ng kuha namin, it's on the size of the particular…

**[02:52:47.680] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002427)  
Building. I mean, the size of that particular area wherein we really do have to make some variation order because of as found nga na po na we need to address.

**[02:53:03.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002428)  
So ano hong status ngayon ng construction po na yan? Naka-hold po?

**[02:53:06.940] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002429)  
No, sir. We are now at the… There are different phases kasi. For the first phase, we're already on 96%.

**[02:53:17.680] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002430)  
For the second phase… No, sorry. For the first phase, it's 90.75%. For the second phase, it's at 96.67%.

**[02:53:37.680] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002431)  
And the last phase, it's at 87.06%. That is as of August…

**[02:53:47.680] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002432)  
Ah, September 30, 2026. And we are looking at… September 30 pala, sorry.

**[02:54:03.120] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002433)  
August 31, 2026. And we are expecting to transfer by January, Mr. Senator Tulfo.

**[02:54:15.220] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002434)  
All right, ma'am. I'm satisfied.

**[02:54:17.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002435)  
I'm satisfied with your answer. Parang kinakabahan ho kayo.

**[02:54:20.060] DEPDev Undersecretary Ma. Monica P. Pagunsan** (asr-002436)  
Kinakabahan ho talaga ako. Kaya I was not expecting that I'm going to answer this question.

**[02:54:25.220] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002437)  
You blame Secretary Balisakan. Anyway, going back to you, Secretary Balisakan, sir.

**[02:54:34.320] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002438)  
Last August 2026, I don't know if this was already asked kanina by my colleagues, the Senate created the Reinvigorate Investment and Sustainable Economic Growth or RISE.

**[02:54:47.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002439)  
Ad-hoc committee to address the recent slowdown in the Philippine economy. I am the vice chair of this ad-hoc committee, Mr. Secretary. May mga nakikita ba kayong legislative reforms and long-term policy interventions aimed at sustainable economic growth?

**[02:55:08.400] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002440)  
We have, Your Honor, the LEDAC, the Reform…

**[02:55:17.680] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002441)  
Our legislative list of LEDAC reflect the priorities of the administration to speed up recovery and sustain economic development, Your Honor.

**[02:55:34.800] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002442)  
It's a long list, but about 23 of those are committed by both houses of Congress, by the leaders of the both houses.

**[02:55:47.680] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002443)  
And the executive to pass by June of next year. Hopefully, we get…

**[02:55:56.060] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002444)  
One of those, for example, is on the energy, the amendment to the EPIRA, Your Honor, to address this high cost of power.

**[02:56:06.280] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002445)  
The others have to do with improving accountability and transparency, access to…

**[02:56:17.680] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002446)  
Information that we believe will help improve the sentiment of our people, particularly the investing community and our economy.

**[02:56:32.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002447)  
Mr. Chair, I mean, Mr. Secretary, is it true because of the economy slowed down last year because of our expenses, government spending, particularly in construction?

**[02:56:47.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002448)  
Dahil nga po, ang center of attention was the flood control by the public works and highways, so nag-slow down.

**[02:56:55.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002449)  
Do you think we will do better this year? I mean, the Senate is funding now construction, I mean, funds for infrastructure by the public works and highways by next year, 2027.

**[02:57:08.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002450)  
Do you think it will greatly help? Because right now, sir, last year or this year, talaga nag-slow down po yung ating economy.

**[02:57:17.680] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002451)  
Definitely, Your Honor, because the last two quarters, at least, saw a sharp contraction of public investments, particularly related to the infrastructure, and that easily cleaved off at least one percentage point growth of GDP.

**[02:57:43.640] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002452)  
It's quite an enormous impact.

**[02:57:47.680] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002453)  
Then if you combine that with the effects of the Middle East conflict, which led to sharp increases in inflation, that substantially tempered down the sentiments of consumers,

**[02:58:09.320] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002454)  
because those combinations of high prices and lower income growth…

**[02:58:17.680] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002455)  
Both meant that their purchasing power was badly affected, and so that, if you look at the consumption side of the economy, also explained the slowdown, because the consumption is a big part of the economy, it's almost a two-third of the economy is just in the consumption, and that slowed down substantially as a result of this weak sentiments of the investment.

**[02:58:47.680] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002456)  
And because private sector infrastructure or private sector investment is also very much related with public sector investment, when the public sector investment collapsed, the private sector was also affected severely.

**[02:59:09.840] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002457)  
So, all this put together led to the situation where we are…

**[02:59:17.680] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002458)  
But I believe that we are starting to recover, Mr. Chair.

**[02:59:30.400] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002459)  
Our colleagues in DBM assured us that the releases of budgets of all agencies and the current pace of disbursements…

**[02:59:47.680] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002460)  
Of contracts, or at least for those that have no, with clear records, could go, and that should…

**[03:00:00.560] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002461)  
allow us to see recovery of construction.

**[03:00:06.220] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002462)  
And you can imagine the amount of construction that could happen

**[03:00:11.440] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002463)  
because last two quarters, as I said,

**[03:00:14.540] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002464)  
we had almost like 30% contraction per quarter.

**[03:00:21.160] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002465)  
And all those unspent money there would have to now be spent

**[03:00:25.100] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002466)  
in the last quarter of, at least in the last quarter of this year,

**[03:00:31.460] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002467)  
plus their budget for this year, for this quarter.

**[03:00:34.820] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002468)  
So you have enormous resources that would have to be mobilized.

**[03:00:39.860] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002469)  
And so if our infrastructure agencies, particularly DPWH,

**[03:00:45.540] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002470)  
but also the DepEd for their school classrooms,

**[03:00:51.820] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002471)  
DA for their farm-to-market roads and irrigation,

**[03:00:55.100] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002472)  
and so on, there's a lot of that, Mr. Chair.

**[03:00:59.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002473)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[03:01:00.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002474)  
Mr. Chair, before I go back to the plenary,

**[03:01:04.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002475)  
I would just like to tell this body that I support

**[03:01:09.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002476)  
for the passage of the budget of the DEP-DEV.

**[03:01:15.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002477)  
And if there is any more or anything that they would like to add on,

**[03:01:20.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002478)  
we will see at the plenary.

**[03:01:22.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002479)  
So I will be supporting, rest assured,

**[03:01:25.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002480)  
I support, Mr. Secretary,

**[03:01:27.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002481)  
for the passage of this budget for 2027 of the DEP-DEV.

**[03:01:32.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002482)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[03:01:34.220] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002483)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:01:35.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002484)  
Thank you, Senator Erwin Tulfo.

**[03:01:39.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002485)  
Anyway, going back to what we discussed,

**[03:01:42.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002486)  
I was interested in the query of Senator Tulfo.

**[03:01:48.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002487)  
That's why this budget season,

**[03:01:52.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002488)  
we really have to accelerate spending and growth.

**[03:01:55.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002489)  
So that we will be able to play catch-up.

**[03:01:57.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002490)  
Talagang malaki-laki nga abuli natin.

**[03:02:01.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002491)  
Is ito, no?

**[03:02:02.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002492)  
Tama, 2% lang tayong GDP this year compared to 4% last year, Secretary?

**[03:02:07.760] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002493)  
4.4 tayo for the full year next year.

**[03:02:12.520] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002494)  
We're still hoping to get at least the low end of our target,

**[03:02:16.960] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002495)  
which is 3.5 to 4.5.

**[03:02:18.620] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002496)  
The low end is 3.5.

**[03:02:19.820] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002497)  
If we can get as close as possible to the lower end,

**[03:02:23.960] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002498)  
I think that will be half.

**[03:02:25.100] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002499)  
Happy because the first two quarters

**[03:02:27.720] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002500)  
are already, you know, continue to be...

**[03:02:31.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002501)  
Talagang grabe effect sa atin nung itong flood control.

**[03:02:36.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002502)  
More than a year already.

**[03:02:39.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002503)  
And we're still reeling from it.

**[03:02:40.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002504)  
Hopefully, baka yung ating public spending

**[03:02:44.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002505)  
will be able to catch up.

**[03:02:48.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002506)  
Sana itong third quarter,

**[03:02:49.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002507)  
pero maka-aabot pa ng third and fourth, no?

**[03:02:52.380] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002508)  
Third quarter siguro.

**[03:02:54.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002509)  
Maka-aabot.

**[03:02:55.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002510)  
Hopefully.

**[03:02:56.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002511)  
Hopefully.

**[03:02:57.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002512)  
That's why yung sabi ko nga dito, Sec,

**[03:02:59.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002513)  
you have to strike a balance right now.

**[03:03:01.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002514)  
Although, of course, we are for accountability

**[03:03:03.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002515)  
dun sa mga nagkasala,

**[03:03:06.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002516)  
the biggest scandal ever.

**[03:03:08.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002517)  
But we need to accelerate the growth also

**[03:03:12.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002518)  
through increased public spending.

**[03:03:16.140] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002519)  
We agree, Mr. Chair.

**[03:03:18.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002520)  
I'll just have a few questions, Secretary.

**[03:03:22.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002521)  
Just take note of the, ano na lang, no?

**[03:03:23.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002522)  
Yung mga...

**[03:03:25.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002523)  
Yung mga issues and concerns of the Senators

**[03:03:30.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002524)  
so that we can prepare for the plenary debates.

**[03:03:35.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002525)  
Malamang baka matanong ulit or may follow-up.

**[03:03:39.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002526)  
So, sa mga agencies concerned,

**[03:03:42.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002527)  
sa mga USEC, ASSEC,

**[03:03:43.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002528)  
please prepare for those.

**[03:03:47.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002529)  
Doon sa Regional Development Councils,

**[03:03:49.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002530)  
ito po, amount of 164 million is appropriated

**[03:03:52.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002531)  
to the Regional Development Councils

**[03:03:54.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002532)  
for the provision of support.

**[03:03:55.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002533)  
Services to RDCs,

**[03:03:57.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002534)  
as well as monitoring and evaluation

**[03:03:59.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002535)  
of the implementation of plans, programs, policies, projects.

**[03:04:04.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002536)  
Given the recent issues involving flood control projects,

**[03:04:07.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002537)  
how can DEP-DEV strengthen the role of Regional Development Councils

**[03:04:11.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002538)  
in the planning process?

**[03:04:13.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002539)  
Not only in identifying and prioritizing infrastructure projects,

**[03:04:17.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002540)  
but also in ensuring that projects are implementation-ready,

**[03:04:22.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002541)  
monitoring of actual implementation,

**[03:04:24.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002542)  
and what projects should continue to receive funding.

**[03:04:29.500] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002543)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:04:31.800] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002544)  
We appreciate the support given to the RDCs last year's budget.

**[03:04:40.120] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002545)  
And we hope that we can get additional resources for the RDCs

**[03:04:45.180] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002546)  
because the RDCs are not DEP-DEV, Mr. Chair.

**[03:04:53.120] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002547)  
We coordinate...

**[03:04:54.040] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002548)  
We are the services secretariat to the RDCs,

**[03:04:56.500] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002549)  
but what constrain the RDCs are the resources available

**[03:05:03.480] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002550)  
for coordination, mobilization,

**[03:05:07.740] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002551)  
and with more resources,

**[03:05:13.540] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002552)  
we could improve the performance of our RDCs,

**[03:05:22.020] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002553)  
particularly in ensuring that...

**[03:05:24.040] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002554)  
that there are enough implementation-ready projects

**[03:05:31.500] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002555)  
identified at the regional level,

**[03:05:34.360] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002556)  
and these are also consistent with the priorities at the national level.

**[03:05:40.260] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002557)  
And as you must have seen,

**[03:05:43.520] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002558)  
the table of Senator Gatchel Jan, Your Honor,

**[03:05:47.940] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002559)  
where he showed that only about 40%, roughly,

**[03:05:54.040] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002560)  
or even less,

**[03:05:56.100] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002561)  
of the high-priority projects of our RDCs

**[03:05:59.620] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002562)  
are reflected in the NEP.

**[03:06:06.520] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002563)  
And then when it comes out,

**[03:06:08.560] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002564)  
it could be even lower or higher,

**[03:06:10.560] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002565)  
depending on who inserted what.

**[03:06:12.860] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002566)  
But we need to do better than that, Your Honor.

**[03:06:17.460] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002567)  
So there's now a coordination,

**[03:06:21.820] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002568)  
a stronger coordination between DEP-DEV,

**[03:06:24.040] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002569)  
and DBM to ensure that the RDC process gets more robust

**[03:06:32.960] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002570)  
so that there is a higher proportion of those projects

**[03:06:36.680] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002571)  
represented in the NEP.

**[03:06:44.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002572)  
Thank you.

**[03:06:45.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002573)  
Still in connection with the Flag of Los Canales,

**[03:06:48.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002574)  
how can we strengthen the coordination between RDC and the DPWH

**[03:06:51.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002575)  
so that projects are properly...

**[03:06:54.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002576)  
revalidated, prioritized,

**[03:06:55.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002577)  
and monitored before government funding is given?

**[03:06:58.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002578)  
Anyway,

**[03:06:59.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002579)  
siguro itong pag-requirement ng RDC,

**[03:07:02.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002580)  
it's the same guy,

**[03:07:05.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002581)  
but at the same time,

**[03:07:05.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002582)  
siguro, Sec,

**[03:07:06.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002583)  
do you think that requiring RDC approval in projects now,

**[03:07:11.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002584)  
at least magkaroon ng tamang direction

**[03:07:13.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002585)  
and we'll spend money wisely,

**[03:07:17.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002586)  
yung funding wisely,

**[03:07:18.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002587)  
na hindi na yung...

**[03:07:20.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002588)  
May iwasan na ba natin yung magkaka-project?

**[03:07:24.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002589)  
Para lang pagkapirahan na...

**[03:07:26.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002590)  
It happens, eh.

**[03:07:27.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002591)  
Sorry, ah.

**[03:07:28.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002592)  
Parang nakikita ko, talaga,

**[03:07:29.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002593)  
I go around and I notice that some...

**[03:07:32.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002594)  
There are some projects that are...

**[03:07:34.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002595)  
Sabi ko nga, hindi naman tayo mayamang bansa,

**[03:07:36.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002596)  
sayang yung project.

**[03:07:38.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002597)  
May mga seawall na pagkalaki-laki,

**[03:07:40.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002598)  
billion yata,

**[03:07:42.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002599)  
na wala namang pinuproteksyon ng community.

**[03:07:44.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002600)  
So, do you think, Secretary,

**[03:07:46.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002601)  
ito pong...

**[03:07:47.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002602)  
By requiring RDC approval,

**[03:07:50.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002603)  
because we're requiring them right now,

**[03:07:52.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002611)  
So, by requiring RDC approval,

**[03:07:56.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002612)  
we will prevent scandals

**[03:08:01.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002613)  
or itong mga projects

**[03:08:02.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002614)  
that are not really needed

**[03:08:05.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002615)  
and yung scams and corruption.

**[03:08:08.360] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002616)  
Yeah.

**[03:08:09.020] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002617)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:08:10.800] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002618)  
We are undertaking a number of initiatives

**[03:08:15.500] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002619)  
to improve the quality

**[03:08:18.960] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002620)  
of our infrastructure projects.

**[03:08:22.740] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002621)  
One is this greater monitoring efforts

**[03:08:32.580] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002622)  
of our, not just DEPDEV,

**[03:08:34.660] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002623)  
but by other agencies as well.

**[03:08:37.520] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002624)  
And with the proper use of technology,

**[03:08:40.040] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002625)  
we can enhance that monitoring.
